<h2>v13.0.1</h2>
<h2><a href="https://github.com/uuidjs/uuid/compare/v13.0.0...v13.0.1">13.0.1</a> (2026-04-27)</h2>
<h3>Bug Fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li>backport fix for GHSA-w5hq-g745-h8pq (<a href="https://github.com/uuidjs/uuid/commit/9d27ddf7046ce496ef39569ff84d948eeff9cb2a">9d27ddf</a>)</li>

<details>
<summary>Changelog</summary>
<p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/uuidjs/uuid/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md">uuid's changelog</a>.</em></p>
<blockquote>
<h2><a href="https://github.com/uuidjs/uuid/compare/v13.0.0...v14.0.0">14.0.0</a> (2026-04-19)</h2>
<h3>Security</h3>
<ul>
<li>Fixes <a href="https://github.com/uuidjs/uuid/security/advisories/GHSA-w5hq-g745-h8pq">GHSA-w5hq-g745-h8pq</a>: <code>v3()</code>, <code>v5()</code>, and <code>v6()</code> did not validate that writes would remain within the bounds of a caller-supplied buffer, allowing out-of-bounds writes when an invalid <code>offset</code> was provided. A <code>RangeError</code> is now thrown if <code>offset &lt; 0</code> or <code>offset + 16 &gt; buf.length</code>.</li>
</ul>
<h3>⚠ BREAKING CHANGES</h3>
<ul>
<li><code>crypto</code> is now expected to be globally defined (requires node@20+) (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/uuidjs/uuid/issues/935">#935</a>)</li>
<li>drop node@18 support (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/uuidjs/uuid/issues/934">#934</a>)</li>
<li>upgrade minimum supported TypeScript version to 5.4.3, in keeping with the project's policy of supporting TypeScript versions released within the last two years</li>
</ul>
</blockquote>
</details>
<details>
<summary>Commits</summary>
<ul>

Updates `ws` from 8.18.3 to 8.20.1
<details>
<summary>Release notes</summary>
<p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/websockets/ws/releases">ws's releases</a>.</em></p>
<blockquote>
<h2>8.20.1</h2>
<h1>Bug fixes</h1>
<ul>
<li>Fixed an uninitialized memory disclosure issue in <code>websocket.close()</code>
(c0327ec1).</li>
</ul>
<p>Providing a <code>TypedArray</code> (e.g. <code>Float32Array</code>) as the <code>reason</code> argument for
<code>websocket.close()</code>, rather than the supported string or <code>Buffer</code> types, caused
uninitialized memory to be disclosed to the remote peer.</p>
<pre lang="js"><code>import { deepStrictEqual } from 'node:assert';
import { WebSocket, WebSocketServer } from 'ws';
<p>const wss = new WebSocketServer(
{ port: 0, skipUTF8Validation: true },
function () {
const { port } = wss.address();
const ws = new WebSocket(<code>ws://localhost:${port}</code>, {
skipUTF8Validation: true
});</p>
<pre><code>ws.on('close', function (code, reason) {
  deepStrictEqual(reason, Buffer.alloc(80));
});
</code></pre>
<p>}
);</p>
<p>wss.on('connection', function (ws) {
ws.close(1000, new Float32Array(20));
});
</code></pre></p>
<p>The issue was privately reported by <a href="https://github.com/ChALkeR">Nikita Skovoroda</a>.</p>
<h2>8.20.0</h2>
<h1>Features</h1>
<ul>
<li>Added exports for the <code>PerMessageDeflate</code> class and utilities for the
<code>Sec-WebSocket-Extensions</code> and <code>Sec-WebSocket-Protocol</code> headers (d3503c1f).</li>
</ul>
<h2>8.19.0</h2>
<h1>Features</h1>
<ul>
<li>Added the <code>closeTimeout</code> option (<a href="https://redirect.github.com/websockets/ws/issues/2308">#2308</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h1>Bug fixes</h1>
<ul>
<li>Handled a forthcoming breaking change in Node.js core (19984854).</li>
</ul>
</blockquote>
</details>

<li><a href="https://github.com/websockets/ws/commit/c0327ec15a54d701eb6ccefaa8bef328cfc03086"><code>c0327ec</code></a> [security] Fix uninitialized memory disclosure in <code>websocket.close()</code></li>

<h3>Greptile Summary</h3>

This is a Dependabot-generated security and maintenance dependency bump across two packages. Both updates address known security vulnerabilities.

- **`uuid` 13.0.0 → 14.0.0** (`packages/cloud-services/operator`, devDependency): Major version bump that drops Node.js 18 support and fixes GHSA-w5hq-g745-h8pq (out-of-bounds writes in `v3()`, `v5()`, and `v6()` when an invalid `offset` is supplied). The operator package already declares `engines: node>=24.0.0`, so the Node.js requirement is satisfied.
- **`ws` 8.18.3 → 8.20.1** (`plugins/plugin-elizacloud`, runtime dependency): Two-minor-version bump within v8 with no breaking changes; patches an uninitialized memory disclosure in `websocket.close()` when a `TypedArray` is passed as the `reason` argument.

<h3>Confidence Score: 5/5</h3>

Safe to merge — both updates are targeted security fixes with no breaking changes affecting the current runtime environment.

The `uuid` bump is a devDependency-only change; the one breaking constraint (Node.js ≥ 20) is already exceeded by the operator's declared engine floor of 24. The `ws` bump stays within the 8.x semver range, carries no breaking changes, and closes an uninitialized memory disclosure. Neither change touches runtime logic, schema, or configuration.

No files require special attention.

<h3>Important Files Changed</h3>

| Filename | Overview |
|----------|----------|
| packages/cloud-services/operator/package.json | Bumps `uuid` from 13.0.0 to 14.0.0 (devDependency only); the breaking node@20+ requirement is already satisfied by the package's `engines: node>=24.0.0` constraint, and the update includes a security fix for GHSA-w5hq-g745-h8pq. |
| plugins/plugin-elizacloud/package.json | Bumps `ws` from 8.18.3 to 8.20.1 (runtime dependency); includes a security fix for an uninitialized memory disclosure in `websocket.close()` and stays within the 8.x semver range with no breaking changes. |

</details>

<h3>Greptile Summary</h3>

Bumps `vite` from `^5.4.0` to `^8.0.13` in the `plugin-xr/simulator` devDependencies, a three-major-version jump that addresses security advisories and picks up bug fixes from v6, v7, and v8.

- The simulator's `vite.config.ts` still uses `rollupOptions`, which Vite 8 has deprecated in favour of `rolldownOptions` (rolldown is now the default bundler); the field still works but will produce deprecation warnings on every build.
- No other breaking changes were identified in the IIFE lib-build configuration used here.

<h3>Confidence Score: 4/5</h3>

Safe to merge — the change is limited to a devDependency version bump and the existing build config continues to work under Vite 8.

The three-major-version jump (5 → 8) is entirely in devDependencies for a build tool. The existing IIFE lib-build config is functional under Vite 8, but `rollupOptions` is now deprecated and will produce build-time warnings until migrated to `rolldownOptions`.

plugins/plugin-xr/simulator/vite.config.ts — the `rollupOptions` key should be updated to `rolldownOptions` to match Vite 8's rolldown bundler.

<h3>Important Files Changed</h3>

| Filename | Overview |
|----------|----------|
| plugins/plugin-xr/simulator/package.json | Bumps vite from ^5.4.0 to ^8.0.13 — a three-major-version jump; the config's `rollupOptions` key is now deprecated in favour of `rolldownOptions` |

<!-- /greptile_comment -->34:Tc8a,# Overall Project Summary: May 19, 2026

Development on May 19 focused on stabilizing the elizaOS live runtime and cloud infrastructure, highlighted by the resolution of critical boot deadlocks and billing record errors. The team also advanced local inference capabilities and build reliability, ensuring better support for both consumer-grade hardware and cloud-based deployments.

## ✅ Completed Work

**Cloud Infrastructure & API Enhancements**
*   Finalized cloud deployment server-side logic, including new status endpoints and deployment triggers ([elizaos/eliza#7804](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/7804)).
*   Resolved cloud container boot deadlocks caused by TLA and barrel-import conflicts ([elizaos/eliza#7801](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/7801)).
*   Addressed HTTP 500 errors in chat completions by making billing audit-row failures non-fatal and improving database error logging ([elizaos/eliza#7794](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/issues/7794)).

**OS Runtime & Build Stability**
*   Improved build reliability by resolving Capacitor CLI paths and enabling support for nested desktop workspace builds ([elizaos/eliza#7799](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/7799), [elizaos/eliza#7798](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/7798)).
*   Fixed packaging blockers for runtime plugins and finalized branding/update materialization ([elizaos/eliza#7800](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/7800), [elizaos/eliza#7797](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/7797)).
*   Configured Flathub-ready builds and hardened USB installer boundaries ([elizaos/eliza#7802](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/7802), [elizaos/eliza#7790](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/7790)).

**Inference & Training Optimization**
*   Introduced a `--low-vram-smoke` CLI preset to enable 2B SFT training on 12GB consumer GPUs ([elizaos/eliza#7805](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/7805)).
*   Enhanced local inference performance by wiring slot save/restore and prewarm functionality via FFI ([elizaos/eliza#7796](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/7796)).

## 🏗️ Work in Progress

**New Pull Requests**
*   [elizaos/eliza#7812](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/7812): Restoring desktop release inputs.
*   [elizaos/eliza#7808](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/7808): Implementing async suspend/resume via job queue for cloud services.
*   [elizaos/eliza#7793](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/7793): CI improvements for Green and Hetzner E2E testing.
*   [elizaos/eliza#7792](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/7792): Fixes for Android local MTP and OmniVoice inference.
*   [elizaos/eliza#7791](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/7791): Salvage of orphaned work from concurrent agent development.

*   [elizaos/eliza#7794](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/issues/7794): Addressed a failure where non-streaming `/v1/chat/completions` returned 500 errors due to billing record insertion issues.
